Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a geometry algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 883. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 15/2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_883(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 883. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^15(S, F) is trivial. QED.
